diff --git a/frontend/src/components/Chart.tsx b/frontend/src/components/Chart.tsx
new file mode 100644

@@ -0,0 +1,293 @@
+import type { ReactNode } from "react";
+import { COLORS, PALETTE } from "../styles/tokens";
+
+export interface ChartPoint {
+  label: string;
+  value: number;
+  tone?: "green" | "red" | "amber" | "slate";
+}
+
+function niceMax(value: number): number {
+  if (value <= 0) return 1;
+  const exponent = Math.floor(Math.log10(value));
+  const magnitude = 10 ** exponent;
+  const normalized = value / magnitude;
+  const nice = normalized <= 1 ? 1 : normalized <= 2 ? 2 : normalized <= 5 ? 5 : 10;
+  return nice * magnitude;
+}
+
+/* =====================================================
+   LINE / AREA CHART
+   ===================================================== */
+
+export function LineChart({
+  data,
+  height = 220,
+  color = COLORS.green700,
+  valueSuffix = "",
+  emptyLabel = "No data for this period.",
+}: {
+  data: ChartPoint[];
+  height?: number;
+  color?: string;
+  valueSuffix?: string;
+  emptyLabel?: string;
+}) {
+  if (data.length === 0) {
+    return <div className="kf-chart-empty">{emptyLabel}</div>;
+  }
+
+  const width = 720;
+  const pad = { top: 18, right: 14, bottom: 34, left: 46 };
+  const innerW = width - pad.left - pad.right;
+  const innerH = height - pad.top - pad.bottom;
+  const maxValue = niceMax(Math.max(...data.map((point) => point.value), 0));
+  const step = data.length > 1 ? innerW / (data.length - 1) : 0;
+  const gradientId = `kf-line-${color.replace("#", "")}`;
+
+  const points = data.map((point, index) => {
+    const x = pad.left + (data.length > 1 ? index * step : innerW / 2);
+    const y = pad.top + innerH - (point.value / maxValue) * innerH;
+    return { ...point, x, y };
+  });
+
+  const path = points.map((point, index) => `${index === 0 ? "M" : "L"} ${point.x} ${point.y}`).join(" ");
+  const area = `${path} L ${points[points.length - 1].x} ${pad.top + innerH} L ${points[0].x} ${pad.top + innerH} Z`;
+  const labelEvery = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(data.length / 6));
+
+  return (
+    <div className="kf-chart">
+      <svg viewBox={`0 0 ${width} ${height}`} role="img" aria-label="Trend line chart">
+        <defs>
+          <linearGradient id={gradientId} x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
+            <stop offset="0%" stopColor={color} stopOpacity="0.22" />
+            <stop offset="100%" stopColor={color} stopOpacity="0.01" />
+          </linearGradient>
+        </defs>
+        {[0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1].map((fraction) => {
+          const y = pad.top + innerH * (1 - fraction);
+          return (
+            <g key={fraction}>
+              <line x1={pad.left} y1={y} x2={width - pad.right} y2={y} stroke={COLORS.chartGrid} strokeWidth="1" />
+              <text x={pad.left - 8} y={y + 4} textAnchor="end" className="kf-chart-axis">
+                {Math.round(maxValue * fraction).toLocaleString()}
+              </text>
+            </g>
+          );
+        })}
+        <path d={area} fill={`url(#${gradientId})`} />
+        <path d={path} fill="none" stroke={color} strokeWidth="2.4" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" />
+        {points.map((point, index) => (
+          <g key={`${point.label}-${index}`}>
+            <circle cx={point.x} cy={point.y} r="3.2" fill={COLORS.white} stroke={color} strokeWidth="2">
+              <title>{`${point.label}: ${point.value.toLocaleString()}${valueSuffix}`}</title>
+            </circle>
+            {index % labelEvery === 0 && (
+              <text x={point.x} y={height - 10} textAnchor="middle" className="kf-chart-axis">
+                {point.label}
+              </text>
+            )}
+          </g>
+        ))}
+      </svg>
+    </div>
+  );
+}
+
+/* =====================================================
+   BAR CHART
+   ===================================================== */
+
+export function BarChart({
+  data,
+  height = 220,
+  valueSuffix = "",
+  emptyLabel = "No data for this period.",
+}: {
+  data: ChartPoint[];
+  height?: number;
+  valueSuffix?: string;
+  emptyLabel?: string;
+}) {
+  if (data.length === 0) {
+    return <div className="kf-chart-empty">{emptyLabel}</div>;
+  }
+
+  const width = 720;
+  const pad = { top: 18, right: 14, bottom: 38, left: 46 };
+  const innerW = width - pad.left - pad.right;
+  const innerH = height - pad.top - pad.bottom;
+  const maxValue = niceMax(Math.max(...data.map((point) => point.value), 0));
+  const slot = innerW / data.length;
+  const barWidth = Math.max(6, Math.min(46, slot * 0.58));
+  const labelEvery = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(data.length / 8));
+
+  const fillFor = (tone: ChartPoint["tone"]) => {
+    if (tone === "red") return COLORS.red;
+    if (tone === "amber") return COLORS.amber;
+    if (tone === "slate") return COLORS.slate;
+    return COLORS.green700;
+  };
+
+  return (
+    <div className="kf-chart">
+      <svg viewBox={`0 0 ${width} ${height}`} role="img" aria-label="Bar chart">
+        {[0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1].map((fraction) => {
+          const y = pad.top + innerH * (1 - fraction);
+          return (
+            <g key={fraction}>
+              <line x1={pad.left} y1={y} x2={width - pad.right} y2={y} stroke={COLORS.chartGrid} strokeWidth="1" />
+              <text x={pad.left - 8} y={y + 4} textAnchor="end" className="kf-chart-axis">
+                {Math.round(maxValue * fraction).toLocaleString()}
+              </text>
+            </g>
+          );
+        })}
+        {data.map((point, index) => {
+          const barHeight = (point.value / maxValue) * innerH;
+          const x = pad.left + slot * index + (slot - barWidth) / 2;
+          const y = pad.top + innerH - barHeight;
+          return (
+            <g key={`${point.label}-${index}`}>
+              <rect x={x} y={y} width={barWidth} height={Math.max(barHeight, 2)} rx="5" fill={fillFor(point.tone)}>
+                <title>{`${point.label}: ${point.value.toLocaleString()}${valueSuffix}`}</title>
+              </rect>
+              {index % labelEvery === 0 && (
+                <text x={x + barWidth / 2} y={height - 12} textAnchor="middle" className="kf-chart-axis">
+                  {point.label}
+                </text>
+              )}
+            </g>
+          );
+        })}
+      </svg>
+    </div>
+  );
+}
+
+/* =====================================================
+   DONUT CHART
+   ===================================================== */
+
+export interface DonutSegment {
+  label: string;
+  value: number;
+  color?: string;
+}
+
+export function DonutChart({
+  segments,
+  centerLabel,
+  centerValue,
+  emptyLabel = "No data recorded.",
+}: {
+  segments: DonutSegment[];
+  centerLabel?: string;
+  centerValue?: ReactNode;
+  emptyLabel?: string;
+}) {
+  const total = segments.reduce((sum, segment) => sum + Math.max(0, segment.value), 0);
+
+  if (total <= 0) {
+    return <div className="kf-chart-empty">{emptyLabel}</div>;
+  }
+
+  const radius = 54;
+  const circumference = 2 * Math.PI * radius;
+  let offset = 0;
+
+  return (
+    <div className="kf-donut">
+      <div className="kf-donut-visual">
+        <svg viewBox="0 0 140 140" role="img" aria-label="Breakdown chart">
+          <circle cx="70" cy="70" r={radius} fill="none" stroke={COLORS.chartTrack} strokeWidth="16" />
+          {segments.map((segment, index) => {
+            const share = Math.max(0, segment.value) / total;
+            const dash = share * circumference;
+            const circle = (
+              <circle
+                key={segment.label}
+                cx="70"
+                cy="70"
+                r={radius}
+                fill="none"
+                stroke={segment.color ?? PALETTE[index % PALETTE.length]}
+                strokeWidth="16"
+                strokeDasharray={`${dash} ${circumference - dash}`}
+                strokeDashoffset={-offset}
+                strokeLinecap="butt"
+                transform="rotate(-90 70 70)"
+              >
+                <title>{`${segment.label}: ${Math.round(share * 100)}%`}</title>
+              </circle>
+            );
+            offset += dash;
+            return circle;
+          })}
+        </svg>
+        <div className="kf-donut-center">
+          {centerValue && <strong>{centerValue}</strong>}
+          {centerLabel && <span>{centerLabel}</span>}
+        </div>
+      </div>
+      <ul className="kf-donut-legend">
+        {segments.map((segment, index) => (
+          <li key={segment.label}>
+            <span className="kf-donut-swatch" style={{ background: segment.color ?? PALETTE[index % PALETTE.length] }} />
+            <span className="kf-donut-label">{segment.label}</span>
+            <span className="kf-donut-value">{Math.round((Math.max(0, segment.value) / total) * 100)}%</span>
+          </li>
+        ))}
+      </ul>
+    </div>
+  );
+}
+
+/* =====================================================
+   HORIZONTAL BARS
+   ===================================================== */
+
+export function HorizontalBars({
+  items,
+  valuePrefix = "",
+  emptyLabel = "No data recorded.",
+}: {
+  items: { label: string; value: number; share?: number | null; color?: string }[];
+  valuePrefix?: string;
+  emptyLabel?: string;
+}) {
+  if (items.length === 0) {
+    return <div className="kf-chart-empty">{emptyLabel}</div>;
+  }
+
+  const max = Math.max(...items.map((item) => item.value), 1);
+
+  return (
+    <ul className="kf-bars">
+      {items.map((item, index) => {
+        const width = Math.max(2, Math.min(100, item.share ?? (item.value / max) * 100));
+        return (
+          <li key={`${item.label}-${index}`}>
+            <div className="kf-bar-head">
+              <span>{item.label}</span>
+              <strong>
+                {valuePrefix}
+                {item.value.toLocaleString()}
+                {item.share !== undefined && item.share !== null ? ` ┬À ${Math.round(item.share)}%` : ""}
+              </strong>
+            </div>
+            <div className="kf-bar-track">
+              <div
+                className="kf-bar-fill"
+                style={{
+                  width: `${width}%`,
+                  background: item.color ?? PALETTE[index % PALETTE.length],
+                }}
+              />
+            </div>
+          </li>
+        );
+      })}
+    </ul>
+  );
+}

+import { useState } from "react";
+import {
+  X,
+  PlusCircle,
+  CheckCircle2,
+  AlertCircle,
+  Coins,
+  TrendingDown,
+  Egg,
+  Wheat,
+  Syringe,
+} from "lucide-react";
+import {
+  addIncome,
+  addExpense,
+  addProduction,
+  addFeeding,
+  addVaccination,
+} from "../services/storage";
+
+interface DailyRecordModalProps {
+  isOpen: boolean;
+  onClose: () => void;
+  onRecordSaved: () => void;
+}
+
+type ActivityCategory = "income" | "expense" | "production" | "feeding" | "vaccination";
+
+export default function DailyRecordModal({
+  isOpen,
+  onClose,
+  onRecordSaved,
+}: DailyRecordModalProps) {
+  const todayStr = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
+  const [activityDate, setActivityDate] = useState(todayStr);
+  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<ActivityCategory>("income");
+
+  // Income state
+  const [incomeCategory, setIncomeCategory] = useState("Egg Sales");
+  const [incomeAmount, setIncomeAmount] = useState("");
+  const [incomeQuantity, setIncomeQuantity] = useState("");
+  const [incomeUnit, setIncomeUnit] = useState("trays");
+  const [incomeDescription, setIncomeDescription] = useState("");
+
+  // Expense state
+  const [expenseCategory, setExpenseCategory] = useState("Poultry Feed");
+  const [expenseAmount, setExpenseAmount] = useState("");
+  const [expenseDescription, setExpenseDescription] = useState("");
+
+  // Production state
+  const [productionType, setProductionType] = useState<"eggs" | "milk">("eggs");
+  const [productionQuantity, setProductionQuantity] = useState("");
+  const [productionUnit, setProductionUnit] = useState("pieces");
+
+  // Feeding state
+  const [feedingAnimalType, setFeedingAnimalType] = useState("Chickens");
+  const [feedingFeedType, setFeedingFeedType] = useState("Layers Mash");
+  const [feedingQuantity, setFeedingQuantity] = useState("");
+  const [feedingUnit, setFeedingUnit] = useState("kg");
+  const [feedingCost, setFeedingCost] = useState("");
+
+  // Vaccination state
+  const [vacSpecies, setVacSpecies] = useState<"poultry" | "cattle">("poultry");
+  const [vacTargetName, setVacTargetName] = useState("");
+  const [vacName, setVacName] = useState("");
+  const [vacDisease, setVacDisease] = useState("");
+  const [vacDose, setVacDose] = useState("");
+
+  // UI state
+  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
+  const [errorMsg, setErrorMsg] = useState("");
+  const [successMsg, setSuccessMsg] = useState("");
+
+  if (!isOpen) return null;
+
+  async function handleSave(andAddAnother: boolean = false) {
+    setErrorMsg("");
+    setSuccessMsg("");
+
+    if (!activityDate) {
+      setErrorMsg("Please select the activity date.");
+      return;
+    }
+
+    try {
+      setSaving(true);
+
+      if (activeTab === "income") {
+        const amt = parseFloat(incomeAmount);
+        if (isNaN(amt) || amt <= 0) {
+          setErrorMsg("Please enter a valid income amount in KES.");
+          setSaving(false);
+          return;
+        }
+        const desc = incomeDescription.trim() || `${incomeCategory} on ${activityDate}`;
+        const qty = parseFloat(incomeQuantity) || 0;
+
+        await addIncome({
+          category: incomeCategory,
+          description: desc,
+          amount: amt,
+          date: activityDate,
+          quantity: qty,
+          unit: incomeUnit,
+        });
+        setSuccessMsg(`Ô£ô Saved income: KES ${amt.toLocaleString()} (${incomeCategory})`);
+        setIncomeAmount("");
+        setIncomeQuantity("");
+        setIncomeDescription("");
+      } else if (activeTab === "expense") {
+        const amt = parseFloat(expenseAmount);
+        if (isNaN(amt) || amt <= 0) {
+          setErrorMsg("Please enter a valid expense amount in KES.");
+          setSaving(false);
+          return;
+        }
+        const desc = expenseDescription.trim() || `${expenseCategory} on ${activityDate}`;
+
+        await addExpense({
+          category: expenseCategory,
+          description: desc,
+          amount: amt,
+          date: activityDate,
+        });
+        setSuccessMsg(`Ô£ô Saved expense: KES ${amt.toLocaleString()} (${expenseCategory})`);
+        setExpenseAmount("");
+        setExpenseDescription("");
+      } else if (activeTab === "production") {
+        const qty = parseFloat(productionQuantity);
+        if (isNaN(qty) || qty <= 0) {
+          setErrorMsg("Please enter a valid production quantity.");
+          setSaving(false);
+          return;
+        }
+
+        await addProduction({
+          type: productionType,
+          quantity: qty,
+          unit: productionUnit,
+          date: activityDate,
+          destination: "produced",
+          sellingPrice: 0,
+          notes: "",
+        });
+        setSuccessMsg(`Ô£ô Recorded production: ${qty} ${productionUnit} of ${productionType}`);
+        setProductionQuantity("");
+      } else if (activeTab === "feeding") {
+        const qty = parseFloat(feedingQuantity);
+        if (isNaN(qty) || qty <= 0) {
+          setErrorMsg("Please enter a valid feed quantity.");
+          setSaving(false);
+          return;
+        }
+        const cost = parseFloat(feedingCost) || 0;
+
+        await addFeeding({
+          animalType: feedingAnimalType,
+          feedType: feedingFeedType,
+          quantity: qty,
+          unit: feedingUnit,
+          date: activityDate,
+          cost,
+          notes: "",
+        });
+        setSuccessMsg(`Ô£ô Recorded feeding: ${qty} ${feedingUnit} ${feedingFeedType}`);
+        setFeedingQuantity("");
+        setFeedingCost("");
+      } else if (activeTab === "vaccination") {
+        if (!vacName.trim()) {
+          setErrorMsg("Please enter the vaccine name.");
+          setSaving(false);
+          return;
+        }
+
+        await addVaccination({
+          species: vacSpecies,
+          targetType: vacSpecies === "poultry" ? "batch" : "animal",
+          targetId: "direct-entry",
+          targetName: vacTargetName.trim() || (vacSpecies === "poultry" ? "Main Flock" : "Herd"),
+          vaccineName: vacName.trim(),
+          disease: vacDisease.trim() || "Preventative",
+          dateAdministered: activityDate,
+          dateDue: activityDate,
+          dose: vacDose.trim(),
+          administeredBy: "Farmer",
+          status: "completed",
+          notes: "Recorded via Daily Activities",
+        });
+        setSuccessMsg(`Ô£ô Saved administered vaccine: ${vacName.trim()}`);
+        setVacName("");
+        setVacDisease("");
+        setVacDose("");
+      }
+
+      onRecordSaved();
+
+      if (!andAddAnother) {
+        setTimeout(() => {
+          onClose();
+        }, 800);
+      }
+    } catch (err) {
+      console.error(err);
+      setErrorMsg(err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Failed to save record.");
+    } finally {
+      setSaving(false);
+    }
+  }
+
+  return (
+    <div className="modal-overlay" role="dialog" aria-modal="true">
+      <div className="modal-card fast-entry-modal">
+        {/* Header */}
+        <div className="modal-header">
+          <div>
+            <h2 className="modal-title">Record Farm Activity</h2>
+            <p className="modal-subtitle">Quickly log income, expenses, feeding or production</p>
+          </div>
+          <button className="modal-close-btn" onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close">
+            <X size={20} />
+          </button>
+        </div>
+
+        {/* Date Selector Row */}
+        <div className="daily-date-picker-row">
+          <label htmlFor="daily-record-date">Activity Date:</label>
+          <input
+            id="daily-record-date"
+            type="date"
+            className="daily-date-input"
+            value={activityDate}
+            max={todayStr}
+            onChange={(e) => setActivityDate(e.target.value)}
+          />
+          {activityDate === todayStr && <span className="today-badge">Today</span>}
+        </div>
+
+        {/* Category Tabs */}
+        <div className="daily-tabs-bar">
+          <button
+            type="button"
+            className={`daily-tab-btn ${activeTab === "income" ? "active" : ""}`}
+            onClick={() => { setActiveTab("income"); setErrorMsg(""); }}
+          >
+            <Coins size={16} /> Income
+          </button>
+          <button
+            type="button"
+            className={`daily-tab-btn ${activeTab === "expense" ? "active" : ""}`}
+            onClick={() => { setActiveTab("expense"); setErrorMsg(""); }}
+          >
+            <TrendingDown size={16} /> Expense
+          </button>
+          <button
+            type="button"
+            className={`daily-tab-btn ${activeTab === "production" ? "active" : ""}`}
+            onClick={() => { setActiveTab("production"); setErrorMsg(""); }}
+          >
+            <Egg size={16} /> Production
+          </button>
+          <button
+            type="button"
+            className={`daily-tab-btn ${activeTab === "feeding" ? "active" : ""}`}
+            onClick={() => { setActiveTab("feeding"); setErrorMsg(""); }}
+          >
+            <Wheat size={16} /> Feeding
+          </button>
+          <button
+            type="button"
+            className={`daily-tab-btn ${activeTab === "vaccination" ? "active" : ""}`}
+            onClick={() => { setActiveTab("vaccination"); setErrorMsg(""); }}
+          >
+            <Syringe size={16} /> Vaccine
+          </button>
+        </div>
+
+        {/* Dynamic Form Content */}
+        <div className="daily-form-body">
+          {errorMsg && (
+            <div className="kf-alert kf-alert--error">
+              <AlertCircle size={16} />
+              <span>{errorMsg}</span>
+            </div>
+          )}
+
+          {successMsg && (
+            <div className="kf-alert kf-alert--success">
+              <CheckCircle2 size={16} />
+              <span>{successMsg}</span>
+            </div>
+          )}
+
+          {/* INCOME TAB */}
+          {activeTab === "income" && (
+            <div className="form-group-stack">
+              <div className="form-row">
+                <div className="form-field">
+                  <label>Income Type</label>
+                  <select
+                    value={incomeCategory}
+                    onChange={(e) => {
+                      setIncomeCategory(e.target.value);
+                      if (e.target.value === "Egg Sales") setIncomeUnit("trays");
+                      else if (e.target.value === "Milk Sales") setIncomeUnit("litres");
+                      else if (e.target.value === "Poultry Sales") setIncomeUnit("birds");
+                      else if (e.target.value === "Cattle Sales") setIncomeUnit("heads");
+                    }}
+                  >
+                    <option value="Egg Sales">Egg Sales</option>
+                    <option value="Milk Sales">Milk Sales</option>
+                    <option value="Poultry Sales">Poultry Sales (Chickens/Broilers)</option>
+                    <option value="Cattle Sales">Cattle / Calf Sales</option>
+                    <option value="Manure Sales">Manure Sales</option>
+                    <option value="Other Farm Income">Other Farm Income</option>
+                  </select>
+                </div>
+                <div className="form-field">
+                  <label>Total Amount Received (KES) *</label>
+                  <input
+                    type="number"
+                    placeholder="e.g. 4500"
+                    value={incomeAmount}
+                    onChange={(e) => setIncomeAmount(e.target.value)}
+                    required
+                  />
+                </div>
+              </div>
+
+              <div className="form-row">
+                <div className="form-field">
+                  <label>Quantity Sold (Optional)</label>
+                  <input
+                    type="number"
+                    placeholder="e.g. 15"
+                    value={incomeQuantity}
+                    onChange={(e) => setIncomeQuantity(e.target.value)}
+                  />
+                </div>
+                <div className="form-field">
+                  <label>Unit</label>
+                  <input
+                    type="text"
+                    placeholder="trays, litres, birds, bags"
+                    value={incomeUnit}
+                    onChange={(e) => setIncomeUnit(e.target.value)}
+                  />
+                </div>
+              </div>
+
+              <div className="form-field">
+                <label>Description / Buyer Notes</label>
+                <input
+                  type="text"
+                  placeholder="e.g. 15 trays sold to local hotel"
+                  value={incomeDescription}
+                  onChange={(e) => setIncomeDescription(e.target.value)}
+                />
+              </div>
+            </div>
+          )}
+
+          {/* EXPENSE TAB */}
+          {activeTab === "expense" && (
+            <div className="form-group-stack">
+              <div className="form-row">
+                <div className="form-field">
+                  <label>Expense Category</label>
+                  <select
+                    value={expenseCategory}
+                    onChange={(e) => setExpenseCategory(e.target.value)}
+                  >
+                    <option value="Poultry Feed">Poultry Feed (Mash / Pellets)</option>
+                    <option value="Cattle Feed">Cattle Feed (Dairy Meal / Hay / Silage)</option>
+                    <option value="Chicks Purchased">Chicks Purchased (Day-old)</option>
+                    <option value="Cattle Purchased">Cattle / Calf Purchased</option>
+                    <option value="Vaccines & Medication">Vaccines & Medication</option>
+                    <option value="Veterinary Services">Veterinary Services</option>
+                    <option value="Farm Labor">Farm Labor</option>
+                    <option value="Transport">Transport & Logistics</option>
+                    <option value="Water & Electricity">Water & Electricity</option>
+                    <option value="Equipment & Repairs">Equipment & Housing Repairs</option>
+                    <option value="Packaging">Packaging (Egg trays, etc.)</option>
+                    <option value="Other Operating Expense">Other Operating Expense</option>
+                  </select>
+                </div>
+                <div className="form-field">
+                  <label>Amount Spent (KES) *</label>
+                  <input
+                    type="number"
+                    placeholder="e.g. 3200"
+                    value={expenseAmount}
+                    onChange={(e) => setExpenseAmount(e.target.value)}
+                    required
+                  />
+                </div>
+              </div>
+
+              <div className="form-field">
+                <label>Description / Supplier</label>
+                <input
+                  type="text"
+                  placeholder="e.g. 2 bags of layers mash from Kagochi Agrovet"
+                  value={expenseDescription}
+                  onChange={(e) => setExpenseDescription(e.target.value)}
+                />
+              </div>
+            </div>
+          )}
+
+          {/* PRODUCTION TAB */}
+          {activeTab === "production" && (
+            <div className="form-group-stack">
+              <div className="form-row">
+                <div className="form-field">
+                  <label>Production Item</label>
+                  <select
+                    value={productionType}
+                    onChange={(e) => {
+                      const t = e.target.value as "eggs" | "milk";
+                      setProductionType(t);
+                      setProductionUnit(t === "eggs" ? "pieces" : "litres");
+                    }}
+                  >
+                    <option value="eggs">­ƒÑÜ Eggs Collected</option>
+                    <option value="milk">­ƒÑø Milk Milked</option>
+                  </select>
+                </div>
+                <div className="form-field">
+                  <label>Quantity *</label>
+                  <input
+                    type="number"
+                    placeholder="e.g. 180"
+                    value={productionQuantity}
+                    onChange={(e) => setProductionQuantity(e.target.value)}
+                    required
+                  />
+                </div>
+                <div className="form-field">
+                  <label>Unit</label>
+                  {productionType === "eggs" ? (
+                    <select
+                      value={productionUnit}
+                      onChange={(e) => setProductionUnit(e.target.value)}
+                    >
+                      <option value="pieces">Pieces (individual eggs)</option>
+                      <option value="trays">Trays (30 eggs/tray)</option>
+                    </select>
+                  ) : (
+                    <input type="text" value="litres" disabled />
+                  )}
+                </div>
+              </div>
+              <p className="field-hint">
+                Note: Record daily collection here. When eggs or milk are sold, also record them under the Income tab.
+              </p>
+            </div>
+          )}
+
+          {/* FEEDING TAB */}
+          {activeTab === "feeding" && (
+            <div className="form-group-stack">
+              <div className="form-row">
+                <div className="form-field">
+                  <label>Animals Fed</label>
+                  <select
+                    value={feedingAnimalType}
+                    onChange={(e) => {
+                      setFeedingAnimalType(e.target.value);
+                      if (e.target.value === "Chickens") setFeedingFeedType("Layers Mash");
+                      else if (e.target.value === "Chicks") setFeedingFeedType("Chick Starter Mash");
+                      else if (e.target.value === "Broilers") setFeedingFeedType("Broiler Finisher");
+                      else if (e.target.value === "Cattle") setFeedingFeedType("Dairy Meal");
+                      else if (e.target.value === "Calves") setFeedingFeedType("Calf Starter Pellets");
+                    }}
+                  >
+                    <option value="Chickens">Chickens / Layers</option>
+                    <option value="Chicks">Day-old Chicks</option>
+                    <option value="Broilers">Broilers</option>
+                    <option value="Cattle">Dairy Cattle / Cows</option>
+                    <option value="Calves">Calves / Heifers</option>
+                  </select>
+                </div>
+                <div className="form-field">
+                  <label>Feed Type</label>
+                  <input
+                    type="text"
+                    value={feedingFeedType}
+                    onChange={(e) => setFeedingFeedType(e.target.value)}
+                    placeholder="e.g. Layers Mash, Dairy Meal, Hay"
+                  />
+                </div>
+              </div>
+
+              <div className="form-row">
+                <div className="form-field">
+                  <label>Quantity Fed *</label>
+                  <input
+                    type="number"
+                    placeholder="e.g. 50"
+                    value={feedingQuantity}
+                    onChange={(e) => setFeedingQuantity(e.target.value)}
+                    required
+                  />
+                </div>
+                <div className="form-field">
+                  <label>Unit</label>
+                  <select
+                    value={feedingUnit}
+                    onChange={(e) => setFeedingUnit(e.target.value)}
+                  >
+                    <option value="kg">Kilograms (kg)</option>
+                    <option value="bags">Bags</option>
+                    <option value="g">Grams (g)</option>
+                    <option value="litres">Litres</option>
+                  </select>
+                </div>
+                <div className="form-field">
+                  <label>Feed Cost (KES, Optional)</label>
+                  <input
+                    type="number"
+                    placeholder="e.g. 2800"
+                    value={feedingCost}
+                    onChange={(e) => setFeedingCost(e.target.value)}
+                  />
+                </div>
+              </div>
+            </div>
+          )}
+
+          {/* VACCINATION TAB */}
+          {activeTab === "vaccination" && (
+            <div className="form-group-stack">
+              <div className="form-row">
+                <div className="form-field">
+                  <label>Animal Species</label>
+                  <select
+                    value={vacSpecies}
+                    onChange={(e) => setVacSpecies(e.target.value as "poultry" | "cattle")}
+                  >
+                    <option value="poultry">­ƒÉö Poultry (Flock / Batch)</option>
+                    <option value="cattle">­ƒÉä Cattle (Cow / Calf / Herd)</option>
+                  </select>
+                </div>
+                <div className="form-field">
+                  <label>Group / Animal Name</label>
+                  <input
+                    type="text"
+                    placeholder={vacSpecies === "poultry" ? "e.g. Batch A (Broilers)" : "e.g. Neema or Entire Herd"}
+                    value={vacTargetName}
+                    onChange={(e) => setVacTargetName(e.target.value)}
+                  />
+                </div>
+              </div>
+
+              <div className="form-row">
+                <div className="form-field">
+                  <label>Vaccine Name *</label>
+                  <input
+                    type="text"
+                    placeholder="e.g. Newcastle Vaccine, Gumboro, ECF"
+                    value={vacName}
+                    onChange={(e) => setVacName(e.target.value)}
+                    required
+                  />
+                </div>
+                <div className="form-field">
+                  <label>Target Disease</label>
+                  <input
+                    type="text"
+                    placeholder="e.g. Newcastle Disease, IBD, FMD"
+                    value={vacDisease}
+                    onChange={(e) => setVacDisease(e.target.value)}
+                  />
+                </div>
+                <div className="form-field">
+                  <label>Dose / Route</label>
+                  <input
+                    type="text"
+                    placeholder="e.g. Eye drop, Drinking water, Injection"
+                    value={vacDose}
+                    onChange={(e) => setVacDose(e.target.value)}
+                  />
+                </div>
+              </div>
+            </div>
+          )}
+        </div>
+
+        {/* Footer Actions */}
+        <div className="modal-actions-footer">
+          <button
+            type="button"
+            className="kf-btn kf-btn--secondary"
+            onClick={onClose}
+            disabled={saving}
+          >
+            Cancel
+          </button>
+          <div className="action-button-group">
+            <button
+              type="button"
+              className="btn-outline-primary"
+              onClick={() => handleSave(true)}
+              disabled={saving}
+            >
+              <PlusCircle size={16} /> Save & Add Another
+            </button>
+            <button
+              type="button"
+              className="kf-btn kf-btn--primary"
+              onClick={() => handleSave(false)}
+              disabled={saving}
+            >
+              {saving ? "Saving..." : "Save Record"}
+            </button>
+          </div>
+        </div>
+      </div>
+    </div>
+  );
+}

+.farm-background__photo {

+.farm-background__cloud--two {

+
+.farm-background__farmhouse {
+  transform-origin: 1160px 530px;
+}
+
+.farm-background__atmosphere {
+  position: absolute;
+  inset: 0;
+  z-index: 1;
+  background:
+    linear-gradient(110deg, rgba(9, 34, 22, .58), rgba(10, 40, 25, .2) 56%, rgba(7, 29, 20, .48)),

+function getInitials(name: string): string {
+  const parts = name.trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
+  if (parts.length === 0) return "?";
+  if (parts.length === 1) return parts[0][0].toUpperCase();
+  return `${parts[0][0]}${parts[parts.length - 1][0]}`.toUpperCase();

+import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
+import type { ReactNode } from "react";
+import { AlertCircle, CheckCircle2, ChevronRight, Loader2, X } from "lucide-react";
+
+/* =====================================================
+   PAGE HEADER
+   ===================================================== */
+
+interface PageHeaderProps {
+  eyebrow?: string;
+  title: string;
+  description?: string;
+  actions?: ReactNode;
+  children?: ReactNode;
+}
+
+export function PageHeader({ eyebrow, title, description, actions, children }: PageHeaderProps) {
+  return (
+    <header className="kf-page-header">
+      <div className="kf-page-header-copy">
+        {eyebrow && <p className="kf-eyebrow">{eyebrow}</p>}
+        <h1 className="kf-page-title">{title}</h1>
+        {description && <p className="kf-page-description">{description}</p>}
+        {children}
+      </div>
+      {actions && <div className="kf-page-actions">{actions}</div>}
+    </header>
+  );
+}
+
+/* =====================================================
+   STAT CARD
+   ===================================================== */
+
+type StatTone = "forest" | "green" | "amber" | "red" | "blue" | "slate" | "plum";
+
+interface StatCardProps {
+  label: string;
+  value: ReactNode;
+  hint?: string;
+  icon?: ReactNode;
+  tone?: StatTone;
+  onClick?: () => void;
+  active?: boolean;
+  trend?: { value: string; direction: "up" | "down" | "flat"; good?: boolean };
+}
+
+export function StatCard({ label, value, hint, icon, tone = "green", onClick, active, trend }: StatCardProps) {
+  const content = (
+    <>
+      <span className="kf-stat-icon">{icon}</span>
+      <span className="kf-stat-body">
+        <span className="kf-stat-label">{label}</span>
+        <span className="kf-stat-value">{value}</span>
+        {hint && <span className="kf-stat-hint">{hint}</span>}
+        {trend && (
+          <span className={`kf-trend kf-trend--${trend.direction}${trend.good === false ? " kf-trend--bad" : ""}`}>
+            {trend.direction === "up" ? "Ôû▓" : trend.direction === "down" ? "Ôû╝" : "ÔÇó"} {trend.value}
+          </span>
+        )}
+      </span>
+      {onClick && <ChevronRight className="kf-stat-chevron" size={16} aria-hidden="true" />}
+    </>
+  );
+
+  const classes = `kf-stat kf-stat--${tone}${onClick ? " kf-stat--interactive" : ""}${active ? " is-active" : ""}`;
+
+  if (onClick) {
+    return (
+      <button type="button" className={classes} onClick={onClick}>
+        {content}
+      </button>
+    );
+  }
+
+  return <div className={classes}>{content}</div>;
+}
+
+/* =====================================================
+   PANEL
+   ===================================================== */
+
+interface PanelProps {
+  title?: string;
+  subtitle?: string;
+  actions?: ReactNode;
+  children: ReactNode;
+  className?: string;
+  id?: string;

+
+export function Panel({ title, subtitle, actions, children, className = "", id }: PanelProps) {
+  return (
+    <section className={`kf-panel ${className}`} id={id}>
+      {(title || actions) && (
+        <div className="kf-panel-header">
+          <div>
+            {title && <h2 className="kf-panel-title">{title}</h2>}
+            {subtitle && <p className="kf-panel-subtitle">{subtitle}</p>}
+          </div>
+          {actions && <div className="kf-panel-actions">{actions}</div>}
+        </div>
+      )}
+      <div className="kf-panel-body">{children}</div>
+    </section>
+  );
+}

+export function EmptyState({ icon, title, description, action, compact }: EmptyStateProps) {

+      <h3>{title}</h3>
+      {description && <p>{description}</p>}
+      {action && <div className="kf-empty-action">{action}</div>}
+    </div>
+  );

+      <span>{label}</span>
+    </div>
+  );

+      <span>{message}</span>
+      {onRetry && (
+        <button type="button" className="kf-alert-action" onClick={onRetry}>
+          Try again
+        </button>
+      )}
+    </div>
+  );

+  children,
+  tone = "success",
+}: {
+  children: ReactNode;
+  tone?: "success" | "warning" | "info";
+}) {
+  return (
+    <div className={`kf-alert kf-alert--${tone}`} role="status">
+      {tone === "success" ? <CheckCircle2 size={18} /> : <AlertCircle size={18} />}
+      <span>{children}</span>
+    </div>
+  );

+  children,
+  tone = "slate",
+  dot,
+}: {
+  children: ReactNode;
+  tone?: BadgeTone;
+  dot?: boolean;
+}) {

+      {children}

+  );

+/* =====================================================
+   BUTTONS
+   ===================================================== */
+
+type ButtonVariant = "primary" | "secondary" | "ghost" | "danger" | "soft";
+
+interface ButtonProps extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
+  variant?: ButtonVariant;
+  size?: "sm" | "md" | "lg";
+  icon?: ReactNode;
+  loading?: boolean;
+  block?: boolean;
+}
+
+export function Button({
+  variant = "primary",
+  size,
+  icon,
+  loading,
+  block,
+  className = "",
+  children,
+  disabled,
+  ...rest
+}: ButtonProps) {
+  return (
+    <button
+      type="button"
+      className={`kf-btn kf-btn--${variant}${block ? " kf-btn--block" : ""}${size && size !== "md" ? " kf-btn--" + size : ""} ${className}`}
+      disabled={disabled || loading}
+      {...rest}
+    >
+      {loading ? <Loader2 size={16} className="kf-spin" /> : icon}
+      {children && <span>{children}</span>}
+    </button>
+  );
+}
+
+export function IconButton({
+  label,
+  icon,
+  onClick,
+  tone = "neutral",
+  disabled,
+}: {
+  label: string;
+  icon: ReactNode;
+  onClick: () => void;
+  tone?: "neutral" | "danger";
+  disabled?: boolean;
+}) {
+  return (
+    <button
+      type="button"
+      className={`kf-icon-btn kf-icon-btn--${tone}`}
+      onClick={onClick}
+      aria-label={label}
+      title={label}
+      disabled={disabled}
+    >
+      {icon}
+    </button>
+  );
+}

+/* =====================================================
+   FIELDS
+   ===================================================== */
+
+interface FieldProps {
+  label: string;
+  htmlFor?: string;
+  hint?: ReactNode;
+  error?: string;
+  required?: boolean;
+  children: ReactNode;
+  className?: string;
+}
+
+export function Field({ label, htmlFor, hint, error, required, children, className = "" }: FieldProps) {
+  return (
+    <div className={`kf-field ${className}`}>
+      <label htmlFor={htmlFor} className="kf-label">
+        {label}
+        {required && <span className="kf-required">*</span>}
+      </label>
+      {children}
+      {error ? <p className="kf-field-error">{error}</p> : hint ? <p className="kf-field-hint">{hint}</p> : null}
+    </div>
+  );

+/* =====================================================
+   SEGMENTED CONTROL
+   ===================================================== */
+
+interface SegmentedOption {
+  value: string;
+  label: string;
+  count?: number;
+  icon?: ReactNode;
+}
+
+export function Segmented({
+  options,
+  value,
+  onChange,
+  label,
+}: {
+  options: SegmentedOption[];
+  value: string;
+  onChange: (value: string) => void;
+  label?: string;
+}) {
+  return (
+    <div className="kf-segmented" role="tablist" aria-label={label}>
+      {options.map((option) => (
+        <button
+          key={option.value}
+          type="button"
+          role="tab"
+          aria-selected={option.value === value}
+          className={`kf-segment${option.value === value ? " is-active" : ""}`}
+          onClick={() => onChange(option.value)}
+        >
+          {option.icon}
+          <span>{option.label}</span>
+          {option.count !== undefined && <span className="kf-segment-count">{option.count}</span>}
+        </button>
+      ))}
+    </div>
+  );
+}

+/* =====================================================
+   MODAL
+   ===================================================== */
+
+interface ModalProps {
+  open: boolean;
+  onClose: () => void;
+  title: string;
+  subtitle?: string;
+  children: ReactNode;
+  footer?: ReactNode;
+  size?: "sm" | "md" | "lg";
+}
+
+export function Modal({ open, onClose, title, subtitle, children, footer, size = "md" }: ModalProps) {
+  const dialogRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
+
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (!open) return;
+    function handleKey(event: KeyboardEvent) {
+      if (event.key === "Escape") {
+        onClose();
+        return;
+      }
+      if (event.key === "Tab" && dialogRef.current) {
+        const focusable = Array.from(
+          dialogRef.current.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(
+            'a[href], button:not([disabled]), input:not([disabled]), select:not([disabled]), textarea:not([disabled]), [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])'
+          )
+        ).filter((el) => !el.closest("[aria-hidden]"));
+        if (focusable.length === 0) return;
+        const first = focusable[0];
+        const last = focusable[focusable.length - 1];
+        if (event.shiftKey) {
+          if (document.activeElement === first) {
+            event.preventDefault();
+            last.focus();
+          }
+        } else {
+          if (document.activeElement === last) {
+            event.preventDefault();
+            first.focus();
+          }
+        }
+      }
+    }
+    document.addEventListener("keydown", handleKey);
+    const previous = document.body.style.overflow;
+    document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
+    return () => {
+      document.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKey);
+      document.body.style.overflow = previous;
+    };
+  }, [open, onClose]);
+
+  if (!open) return null;
+
+  return (
+    <div className="kf-modal-overlay" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label={title} onMouseDown={onClose}>
+      <div
+        ref={dialogRef}
+        className={`kf-modal kf-modal--${size}`}
+        onMouseDown={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
+      >
+        <div className="kf-modal-header">
+          <div>
+            <h2>{title}</h2>
+            {subtitle && <p>{subtitle}</p>}
+          </div>
+          <IconButton label="Close dialog" icon={<X size={18} />} onClick={onClose} />
+        </div>
+        <div className="kf-modal-body">{children}</div>
+        {footer && <div className="kf-modal-footer">{footer}</div>}
+      </div>
+    </div>
+  );
+}
+
+/* =====================================================
+   HELPERS
+   ===================================================== */
+
+export function InlineMeta({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
+  return <p className="kf-inline-meta">{children}</p>;
+}
+
+export function Divider() {
+  return <div className="kf-divider" role="separator" />;
+}
+
+export function KeyValue({ label, value }: { label: string; value: ReactNode }) {
+  return (
+    <div className="kf-keyvalue">
+      <span>{label}</span>
+      <strong>{value}</strong>
+    </div>
+  );
+}
+
+/* =====================================================
+   SPINNER
+   ===================================================== */
+
+export function Spinner({ size = "md" }: { size?: "sm" | "md" | "lg" }) {
+  const px = size === "sm" ? 16 : size === "lg" ? 28 : 20;
+  return <Loader2 size={px} className="kf-spin" aria-hidden="true" />;
+}
+
+/* =====================================================
+   CSS BAR
+   ===================================================== */
+
+interface CssBarProps {
+  label: string;
+  value: number;
+  max: number;
+  formatted: string;
+  tone?: "green" | "red" | "amber" | "blue";
+}
+
+export function CssBar({ label, value, max, formatted, tone = "green" }: CssBarProps) {
+  const pct = max > 0 ? Math.min(100, (value / max) * 100) : 0;
+  const fillClass = `kf-bar-fill${tone !== "green" ? " kf-bar-fill--" + tone : ""}`;
+  return (
+    <div className="kf-bar-row">
+      <span className="kf-bar-label" title={label}>{label}</span>
+      <div className="kf-bar-track">
+        <div className={fillClass} style={{ width: `${pct}%` }} />
+      </div>
+      <span className="kf-bar-value">{formatted}</span>
+    </div>
+  );
+}
+
+/* =====================================================
+   CARD
+   ===================================================== */
+
+interface CardProps {
+  title?: ReactNode;
+  subtitle?: ReactNode;
+  actions?: ReactNode;
+  footer?: ReactNode;
+  children: ReactNode;
+  className?: string;
+}
+
+export function Card({ title, subtitle, actions, footer, children, className = "" }: CardProps) {
+  return (
+    <div className={`kf-panel ${className}`}>
+      {(title || actions) && (
+        <div className="kf-panel-header">
+          <div>
+            {title && <h2 className="kf-panel-title">{title}</h2>}
+            {subtitle && <p className="kf-panel-subtitle">{subtitle}</p>}
+          </div>
+          {actions && <div className="kf-panel-actions">{actions}</div>}
+        </div>
+      )}
+      <div className="kf-panel-body">{children}</div>
+      {footer && <div className="kf-panel-footer">{footer}</div>}
+    </div>
+  );
+}

+export async function loadFarmSummary(options?: {
+  days?: number;
+  to?: string;
+  from?: string;
+}): Promise<FarmSummaryData> {
+  const params = new URLSearchParams();
+  if (options?.days) params.set("days", String(options.days));
+  if (options?.to) params.set("to", options.to);
+  if (options?.from) params.set("from", options.from);
+  const query = params.toString() ? `?${params.toString()}` : "";
+  const data = await apiRequest<{ summary: FarmSummaryData }>(`/api/farm/summary${query}`);
+  return data.summary;
 }

diff --git a/frontend/src/utils/format.ts b/frontend/src/utils/format.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..089bd12
--- /dev/null
+++ b/frontend/src/utils/format.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,53 @@
+export function todayISO(): string {
+  return new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
+}
+
+export function isoDaysAgo(days: number): string {
+  return new Date(Date.now() - days * 86_400_000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
+}
+
+export function formatNumber(value: number | null | undefined): string {
+  if (value === null || value === undefined || Number.isNaN(value)) return "0";
+  return Number(value).toLocaleString();
+}
+
+export function formatCurrency(value: number | null | undefined): string {
+  return `KES ${Math.round(Number(value) || 0).toLocaleString()}`;
+}
+
+export function formatDate(value?: string | null): string {
+  if (!value) return "ÔÇö";
+  const date = new Date(`${value.slice(0, 10)}T00:00:00`);
+  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return value.slice(0, 10);
+  return date.toLocaleDateString("en-GB", {
+    day: "2-digit",
+    month: "short",
+    year: "numeric",
+  });
+}
+
+export function formatShortDate(value: string): string {
+  const date = new Date(`${value.slice(0, 10)}T00:00:00`);
+  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return value;
+  return date.toLocaleDateString("en-GB", { day: "2-digit", month: "short" });
+}
+
+export function dateRange(from: string, to: string): string[] {
+  const dates: string[] = [];
+  const start = new Date(`${from}T00:00:00`);
+  const end = new Date(`${to}T00:00:00`);
+  if (Number.isNaN(start.getTime()) || Number.isNaN(end.getTime())) return dates;
+  const cursor = new Date(start);
+  let guard = 0;
+  while (cursor <= end && guard < 400) {
+    dates.push(cursor.toISOString().slice(0, 10));
+    cursor.setDate(cursor.getDate() + 1);
+    guard += 1;
+  }
+  return dates;
+}
+
+export function percent(value: number | null | undefined, digits = 1): string {
+  if (value === null || value === undefined || Number.isNaN(value)) return "ÔÇö";
+  return `${Number(value).toFixed(digits)}%`;
+}

+function timeOfDayGreeting(): "morning" | "afternoon" | "evening" {
+  const hour = new Date().getHours();
+  if (hour >= 5 && hour <= 11) return "morning";
+  if (hour >= 12 && hour <= 17) return "afternoon";
+  return "evening";
+}

+  const [customFrom, setCustomFrom] = useState("");
+  const [customTo, setCustomTo] = useState("");
+  const [summary, setSummary] = useState<FarmSummaryData | null>(null);

+  const [error, setError] = useState("");
+  const [modalOpen, setModalOpen] = useState(false);
+
+  const todayStr = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
+
+  const filterParams = useMemo(() => {
+    if (filterKey === "today") return { days: 1, to: todayStr, from: todayStr };
+    if (filterKey === "yesterday") {
+      const yDate = new Date(Date.now() - 86_400_000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
+      return { days: 1, to: yDate, from: yDate };
+    }
+    if (filterKey === "7d") return { days: 7, to: todayStr };
+    if (filterKey === "30d") return { days: 30, to: todayStr };
+    if (filterKey === "month") return { to: todayStr, from: `${todayStr.slice(0, 7)}-01` };
+    if (filterKey === "year") return { to: todayStr, from: `${todayStr.slice(0, 4)}-01-01` };
+    if (filterKey === "custom" && customFrom && customTo) return { from: customFrom, to: customTo };
+    return { days: 30, to: todayStr };
+  }, [filterKey, customFrom, customTo, todayStr]);
+

+      try {
+        setLoading(true);
+        setError("");

+        if (active) setSummary(data);
+      } catch (err) {
+        console.error("Failed to load farm summary:", err);

+        if (active) setLoading(false);

+type CategoryTone = "amber" | "green" | "forest" | "blue" | "slate";

+function categoryBadgeTone(cat: string): CategoryTone {
+  switch (cat) {
+    case "calf": return "amber";
+    case "heifer": return "green";
+    case "cow": return "forest";
+    case "bull": return "blue";

+// Map status ÔåÆ Badge tone
+type BadgeTone = "green" | "amber" | "red" | "blue" | "slate";
+
+function statusTone(status: string): BadgeTone {
+  if (status === "completed") return "green";
+  if (status === "overdue") return "red";
+  if (status === "due_today" || status === "due") return "amber";
+  if (status === "upcoming" || status === "scheduled") return "blue";
+  return "slate";
+}

+// A small table component for a vaccination group
+function VaccGroup({
+  title,
+  tone,
+  vaccs,
+  onMarkDone,
+  onDelete,
+}: {
+  title: string;
+  tone: BadgeTone;
